Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 19, Step 4, "Any other person (other than your spouse) who lived with you all year as a member of your household" (the relationship enum's household_member answer already carries the local-law condition); i1040gi p. 21, Married person: "If the person is married and files a joint return, you can't claim that person as your dependent." executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/dependent.json`; 1,411 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,809 partial records; 8,275 comparisons): 7,623 of 8,275 agree; 234 project-refuses, 157 project-unknown, 141 candidate-unknown, 119 value, 1 candidate-refuses; on returns the full interview completes: 55 value, 30 candidate-unknown, 1 project-unknown. The qualifying-child and qualifying-relative tests are the project's, including the T-091 inputs (dependent.spouse_relative on a joint return only, dependent.qr_all_year for household_member, other and sibling_descendant); multiple_support is now refused only on the qualifying-relative test, as the project does. Every completed-return difference was enumerated with the witness cap lifted (triage.mjs run directly; scratch `adj-t091r2-dep-transcription/dependent.full.json`, causes assigned by `classify2.py`, none unassigned; the uncapped rerun reproduces the recorded counts exactly): (a) 55 value, project yes and candidate no, all contradictory records with married no and joint_return yes (dependent.married asks "... or is a joint return being filed for 2025 that includes this person", so joint_return yes means married yes, which the project refuses at the Married person screen): the reply adds joint_return no to its qualifying-relative test, which the project leaves to that screen, so the two agree on every consistent record (8 fixtures, 6 generated, 41 partial records; for example classm.f8889.1 dep_b, classm.dependent.4 dep_a, generated 436 dep_a); (b) 2 candidate-unknown dependent.lawful on classm.clcw26.2 and .deepseek dep_b, a household_member whose fixture predates that question: the reply asks lawful of a household_member, whose relationship answer already states the local-law condition (an answer the source does not need); (c) 28 candidate-unknown partial records withholding an answer the reply reads first (19 joint_return, 6 kidnapped, 2 multiple_support, 1 lawful); (d) 1 project-unknown partial record of classm.eic.3.deepseek dep_c withholding dependent.tin, which the claimed_no_tin screen reads. Off completed returns: 234 project-refuses, all cited screens of dependent.claimed on returns refused anyway (207 claimed_no_tin, 22 married, 5 income_exception); 1 candidate-refuses: the reply declares dependent.kidnapped on its qualifying-child helper, evaluated for every person, so it refuses t091.kidnapped.age19 (aged 19, incomplete) where p501 p. 18 ends the treatment "the year the child would have reached age 18"; 64 value off completed returns, 63 of them the same contradictory married/joint_return records and classm.sc.2 dep_b (the joint-return route against sa.dependent, a refused return, as in earlier rounds). Model error on (b). Project stands.

Second transcription's note: Item 1 excludes a dependent only because they lived with you all year (relationship household_member, other or sibling_descendant). Multiple-support dependents and the divorced-parent rule are refused. Item 2 is an unmarried qualifying child who isn't your dependent. Item 3 is read as a married qualifying child who would be your dependent except that you can be claimed (citizenship met and not claimed). The absence check is a separate line.

Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 14, Head of Household, Test 2; p501 p. 9, Temporary absences: "It must be reasonable to assume the absent person will return to the home after the temporary absence. You must continue to keep up the home during the absence."; p501 p. 9, Death or birth: "Your qualifying child or qualifying relative who lived with you for more than half the part of the year they were alive". executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/dependent.json`; 1,411 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,809 partial records; 8,275 comparisons): 7,469 of 8,275 agree; 472 project-unknown, 234 project-refuses, 80 candidate-unknown, 20 value; on returns the full interview completes: 82 project-unknown, 23 candidate-unknown, 7 value. The reply's per-person test (an unmarried qualifying child, a dependent related as listed who lived with you more than half the year, or a qualifying child you could claim but for being claimable) is the project's hoh_test2_lived on every consistent completed record, and its note leaves the home cost to household.home_cost. Two defects by reading, both shown off completed returns: (1) it does not exclude a person who reaches more than half the year only by counting an absence head of household does not count ("The absence check is a separate line"), so with the project's household.test2_persons it would meet Test 2 on t091.absence.hoh_refused, t091.absence.other_tests_unchanged and t091r.absence.second_child_clean dep child (project no, candidate yes), where p501 p. 9 says the absence does not count; (2) it reads months of 7 or more only, with no part-year route, so stage8.hoh.parent.died_lived_part_year, stage8.hoh.adopted_part_year and stage15.f2441.parent_died_part_year (project yes, candidate no) lose Test 2. Every completed-return difference was enumerated with the witness cap lifted (triage.mjs run directly; scratch `adj-t091r2-dep-transcription/dependent.full.json`, causes assigned by `classify2.py`, none unassigned; the uncapped rerun reproduces the recorded counts exactly): (a) 7 value, project yes and candidate no, all contradictory records with married no and joint_return yes (dependent.married asks "... or is a joint return being filed for 2025 that includes this person", so joint_return yes means married yes, which the project refuses at the Married person screen): the reply adds joint_return no to its qualifying-relative test, which the project leaves to that screen, so the two agree on every consistent record (classm.f8889.1 and .deepseek dep_b, classm.sli.3 dep_a, stage21.f1.lower_bound_still_decides dep_b, generated 235, 436 and 445); (b) 52 project-unknown dependent.born_died (hoh_test2_lived's part-year route for an entry with months 6 or fewer), which no completed return needs: 48 on returns not filing head of household (classm.f8962.5 and .deepseek dep_b, generated 22, 64, 65, 361, 405, 494, and 40 partial records) and 4 head-of-household returns decided by Test 1 or another Test 2 person (generated 100, 359, 381, 402; eval: household.test2_persons is unknown with lower bound 1 or Test 1 is met, household.hoh_absence no, line 12e computed); this read is unchanged from the pre-T-091 hoh_test2, now hoh_test2_lived; (c) 30 project-unknown partial records (27 withholding absence_no_return, which the interview asks wherever Test 2 is reached, 2 married, 1 months); (d) 23 candidate-unknown partial records (15 joint_return, 5 kidnapped, 2 multiple_support, 1 canada_mexico). Off completed returns: 234 project-refuses (cited screens of dependent.claimed). Project stands.

Second transcription's note: Persons who would be dependents except for gross income, a joint return, or your being claimable are handled by f2441.nondependent, outside this entry. For an incapable person born or who died in 2025, the months input is used as given.

Ruling: model error; i2441 p. 1, Qualifying Person(s): "1. A qualifying child under age 13 whom you can claim as a dependent ... 3. Any disabled person who wasn't physically or mentally able to care for themselves who lived with you for more than half the year and whom you can claim as a dependent"; p503 p. 3, Birth or death of otherwise qualifying person. executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/dependent.json`; 1,411 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,809 partial records; 8,275 comparisons): 4,957 of 8,275 agree; 2,124 value, 950 candidate-unknown, 235 candidate-refuses, 8 project-unknown, 1 project-refuses; on returns the full interview completes: 840 value, 27 candidate-unknown. The reply has no f2441.care condition, so it tests every entry and asks dc_incapable or dc_under13 where no care was paid, and its disabled route reads months of 7 or more with no death-year route: stage15.f2441.parent_died_part_year (a disabled parent who died in 2025, $4,000.00 of care; project yes, Form 2441 line 11 $600.00) is not a qualifying person. Every completed-return difference was enumerated with the witness cap lifted (triage.mjs run directly; scratch `adj-t091r2-dep-transcription/dependent.full.json`, causes assigned by `classify2.py`, none unassigned; the uncapped rerun reproduces the recorded counts exactly): (a) 840 value, project no and candidate yes, all with f2441.care no (59 fixtures, 27 generated, 754 partial records; for example classm.f4952.1.deepseek dep_c), reaching no amount since Form 2441 is not filed; (b) 1 candidate-unknown dc_incapable with care no (classm.scveh.3 dep_a); (c) 26 candidate-unknown partial records (21 dc_incapable, 1 each dc_under13, canada_mexico, divorced_rule, months, self_support). Off completed returns: 235 candidate-refuses (other_claimant, divorced_rule, kidnapped, multiple_support on returns the project refuses), the care omission again, and the death-year case above. Second transcription's note: A Count cannot come from choose, so it is derived with percent_floor. One cent over 100 cents times 100 gives 1, and zero gives 0. A qualifying person counts toward the $6,000 limit even with no expenses.

Second transcription's note: dc_paid includes expenses paid with dependent care benefits. Form 2441 (line 28 against line 30) removes excluded benefits, so they are not subtracted per person here.

The reply's column (d) is dc_paid for its qualifying person, else zero; the project copies dc_paid, and f2441.paid_total sums only entries with dependent.dc_qp. The line inherits the reply's dependent.dc_qp, including its missing death-year route (stage15.f2441.parent_died_part_year: project $4,000.00, candidate $0.00, an incomplete return). Every completed-return difference was enumerated with the witness cap lifted (triage.mjs run directly; scratch `adj-t091r2-dep-transcription/dependent.full.json`, causes assigned by `classify2.py`, none unassigned; the uncapped rerun reproduces the recorded counts exactly): (a) 20 project-unknown dependent.dc_paid on classm.sc.4 dep_a (f2441.care no) and 19 partial records of it, which the interview does not ask (no amount); (b) 25 project-unknown partial records withholding dc_paid; (c) 26 candidate-unknown partial records as dependent.dc_qp. Ruling: model error; i8863 p. 2, Who Can Claim an Education Credit: "You may be able to claim an education credit if you, your spouse, or a dependent you claim on your tax return was a student"; i8863 p. 3, item 5: "Filers and students must have been issued a TIN by the due date of their 2025 return (including extensions)" (amb.2025.edu_filer_tin); i8863 p. 9, Line 31. executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/dependent.json`; 1,411 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,809 partial records; 8,275 comparisons): 4,820 of 8,275 agree; 3,202 candidate-unknown, 246 candidate-refuses, 6 project-refuses, 1 project-unknown; on returns the full interview completes: 758 candidate-unknown. Its lifetime learning line takes every claimed student with expenses whom its American opportunity test rejects; with no filer-TIN or f8863.paid test it gives line 31 $0.00 in the jack_kay rerun above where the project gives $4,300.00. Every completed-return difference was enumerated with the witness cap lifted (triage.mjs run directly; scratch `adj-t091r2-dep-transcription/dependent.full.json`, causes assigned by `classify2.py`, none unassigned; the uncapped rerun reproduces the recorded counts exactly): (a) 741 candidate-unknown dependent.edu on returns with f8863.paid no, where the project hides the line and does not ask it (69 fixtures and 672 partial records; for example classm.f4952.1.deepseek dep_a); (b) 2 candidate-unknown dependent.lawful on classm.clcw26.2 and .deepseek dep_b, a household_member whose fixture predates that question: the reply asks lawful of a household_member, whose relationship answer already states the local-law condition (an answer the source does not need); (c) 15 candidate-unknown partial records (11 joint_return, 3 kidnapped, 1 edu_over of classm.s3.2 dep_a). Off completed returns: 247 candidate-refuses (other_claimant, divorced_rule, kidnapped and multiple_support declared on the reply's claim helpers, and h_edu_over on stage19.refuse.period_over_dependent), all on returns the project refuses; 6 project-refuses (edu_over_refused). Project stands.

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: dependent.one
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One (this person, counted once)"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: param, ref: dependent.one}

  - id: dependent.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}

  - id: dependent.h_cent
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One cent (used to count this person once)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sc.one_cent}

  - id: dependent.h_rel_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 1 relationship: son, daughter, stepchild, foster child, brother, sister, stepbrother, stepsister, half brother, half sister, or a descendant of any of them"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "child"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling_descendant"}]}

  - id: dependent.h_age_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Under age 19 and younger than you (or your spouse if filing jointly); or under age 24, a full-time student, and younger than you; or any age and permanently and totally disabled"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: qc.age_limit}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.younger, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: qc.student_age_limit}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.student, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.younger, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.disabled, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_lived_half
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Lived with you for more than half of 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, ge: [dependent.months, {const: 7, type: Count}]}
    note: "The months input already applies the exceptions for temporary absences and for a person born, who died, or who was adopted or placed in 2025. More than half the year is read as 7 or more months, as the input instructs."

  - id: dependent.h_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 1: a child who meets the conditions to be your qualifying child"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_rel_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_age_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.self_support, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.joint_return, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_lived_half, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [dependent.other_claimant, dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.kidnapped]

  - id: dependent.h_citizen_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "U.S. citizen, U.S. national, U.S. resident alien, or a resident of Canada or Mexico"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.canada_mexico, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_filer_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Filing a joint return for 2025, or you could not be claimed as a dependent on someone else's 2025 return"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [sa.dependent, {const: false}]}
    note: "Step 2 question 3 and Step 4 question 4 let you claim the person on a joint return without asking question 4/5. On any other return, sa.dependent stands for 'Could you be claimed as a dependent on someone else's return'."

  - id: dependent.h_qc_dep
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 2: your qualifying child is your dependent"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_citizen_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_filer_ok, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_rel_qr_strong
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4 relative who doesn't have to live with you"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "child"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent_relative"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "in_law"}]}

  - id: dependent.h_rel_qr
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4 member of household or relationship test"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_rel_qr_strong, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.spouse_relative, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "household_member"}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "other"}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling_descendant"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.qr_all_year, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.lawful, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_qr_dep
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4: your qualifying relative is your dependent"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_rel_qr, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [dependent.gross_income, {param: qr.gross_income_limit}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.supported, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_citizen_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.joint_return, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_filer_ok, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [dependent.multiple_support]
    note: "Not a qualifying child of any other taxpayer is covered by the other_claimant refusal on the qualifying-child helper. The exception to the gross income test for sheltered-workshop income is left to the gross income input."

  - id: dependent.claimed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You can claim this person as a dependent"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_dep, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr_dep, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.ctc_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3: your qualifying child qualifies you for the child tax credit"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_dep, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.tin, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: ctc.age_limit}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.work_ssn, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.ctc_ssn, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.odc_person
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Steps 3 and 5: this dependent qualifies you for the credit for other dependents"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.tin, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.all_tin, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: false}]}

  - id: dependent.h_ctc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$2,200 per qualifying child"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ctc.per_child}

  - id: dependent.ctc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule 8812 line 5 share for this child ($2,200)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_ctc}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}

  - id: dependent.h_odc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$500 per other dependent"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: odc.per_dependent}

  - id: dependent.odc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule 8812 line 7 share for this dependent ($500)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.odc_person, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_odc}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.odc_person, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}

  - id: dependent.h_actc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$1,700 per qualifying child"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: actc.per_child}

  - id: dependent.actc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule 8812 line 16b share for this child ($1,700)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_actc}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}

  - id: dependent.hoh_test2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Head of household Test 2: a person who lived in your home more than half the year and qualifies you"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_dep, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr_dep, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_rel_qr_strong, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_lived_half, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: false}]}
            - op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.married, {const: false}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_citizen_ok, {const: true}]}
    note: "Item 1 excludes a dependent only because they lived with you all year (relationship household_member, other or sibling_descendant). Multiple-support dependents and the divorced-parent rule are refused. Item 2 is an unmarried qualifying child who isn't your dependent. Item 3 is read as a married qualifying child who would be your dependent except that you can be claimed (citizenship met and not claimed). The absence check is a separate line."

  - id: dependent.hoh_absence
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Head of household: time counted as lived with you includes an absence from which return was not reasonable to assume, or during which the home was not kept up"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.hoh_test2, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.absence_no_return, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.hoh_test1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Head of household Test 1: your parent whom you can claim as a dependent, whose main home for all of 2025 you paid over half the cost of keeping up"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.parent_home_paid, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.eic_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Step 3: a child who meets the conditions to be your qualifying child for the EIC"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_rel_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_age_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.joint_return, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.us_half, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.married, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [dependent.other_claimant, dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.kidnapped]
    note: "There is no support test for the EIC. Under the married child rule, a married child is a qualifying child only if you can claim the child as your dependent."

  - id: dependent.eic_ssn_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC qualifying child who has an SSN valid for the EIC"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_child, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_ssn, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_under13
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Under age 13 for part of 2025 when the care was provided"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_under13, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.dc_qp
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 2441 qualifying person: a qualifying child under age 13 whom you can claim as a dependent, or a dependent who wasn't able to care for themselves and lived with you more than half the year"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_dep, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_under13, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_incapable, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_lived_half, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
    note: "Persons who would be dependents except for gross income, a joint return, or your being claimable are handled by f2441.nondependent, outside this entry. For an incapable person born or who died in 2025, the months input is used as given."

  - id: dependent.dc_line2d
    printed: "2(d)"
    label: "Qualified expenses you incurred and paid in 2025 for the person listed in column (a)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.dc_paid}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}
    note: "dc_paid includes expenses paid with dependent care benefits. Form 2441 (line 28 against line 30) removes excluded benefits, so they are not subtracted per person here."

  - id: dependent.dc_line2c
    printed: "2(c)"
    label: "Check here if the qualifying person was over age 12 and was disabled"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_incapable, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, gt: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_over12_care, {const: true}]}
    note: "Someone 14 or older at the end of 2025 was over 12 all year. Someone who is 13 at year end depends on whether the included care was provided after the 13th birthday (dc_over12_care)."

  - id: dependent.h_dc_cent
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One cent if this person is a qualifying person, else zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_cent}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}

  - id: dependent.dc_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of qualifying persons this entry contributes (1 or 0)"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: percent_floor, part: dependent.h_dc_cent, whole: {param: sc.hundred_cents}, cap: 1}
    note: "A Count cannot come from choose, so it is derived with percent_floor. One cent over 100 cents times 100 gives 1, and zero gives 0. A qualifying person counts toward the $6,000 limit even with no expenses."

  - id: dependent.ptc_magi_counted
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet 1-2: this dependent's modified AGI, only if the dependent is required to file because of income"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [dependent.ptc_files, {const: true}]}
          then: dependent.ptc_magi
        - guard:
            op: test
            not:
              op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.ptc_files, {const: true}]}
          then: dependent.h_zero

  - id: dependent.h_edu_over
    printed: "(gate)"
    label: "Tax-free assistance and refunds exceeded an academic period's expenses (per-period allocation not computed)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_over, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_edu_aoc_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Student qualifies for the American opportunity credit (lines 23-26, Form 1098-T, TIN, EIN)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_1098t, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.tin, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line23, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line24, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line25, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line26, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_ein, {const: true}]}
    note: "A dependent's expenses are treated as paid by you only when you claim the student. Without the EIN the American opportunity credit can't be claimed, and the student goes to the lifetime learning credit."

  - id: dependent.h_edu_llc_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Student's expenses go to line 31 (lifetime learning credit)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_1098t, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.tin, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_aoc_ok, {const: false}]}

  - id: dependent.h_edu_aoc_exp
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "AQEE Worksheet line 1 (AOC): tuition, required fees and course materials"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [dependent.edu_inst, dependent.edu_mat]}

  - id: dependent.h_edu_adj
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "AQEE Worksheet line 3: total adjustments (lines 2a, 2b and 2c)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [dependent.edu_a2025, dependent.edu_a2026, dependent.edu_refunds]}

  - id: dependent.h_edu_aoc_adjusted
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "AQEE Worksheet line 4 (AOC): subtract line 3 from line 1; if zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: dependent.h_edu_aoc_exp, take: dependent.h_edu_adj}
    note: "The worksheet is per academic period. With the over-allocation case refused, periods are aggregated."

  - id: dependent.h_edu_llc_adjusted
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "AQEE Worksheet line 4 (LLC): expenses required to be paid to the institution less adjustments; if zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: dependent.edu_inst, take: dependent.h_edu_adj}

  - id: dependent.h_aoc_cap
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$4,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: aoc.expense_cap}

  - id: dependent.h_aoc_band
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$2,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: aoc.full_band}

  - id: dependent.h_edu_aoc_capped
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses, not more than $4,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [dependent.h_edu_aoc_adjusted, dependent.h_aoc_cap]}

  - id: dependent.edu_line27
    printed: "27"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (see instructions). Don't enter more than $4,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_aoc_ok, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_edu_aoc_capped}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_aoc_ok, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}
    refuses: [dependent.h_edu_over]

  - id: dependent.edu_line28
    printed: "28"
    label: "Subtract $2,000 from line 27. If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: dependent.edu_line27, take: {param: aoc.full_band}}

  - id: dependent.edu_line29
    printed: "29"
    label: "Multiply line 28 by 25% (0.25)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: dependent.edu_line28, rate: {param: aoc.second_rate}}

  - id: dependent.h_edu_first
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Smaller of line 27 or $2,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [dependent.edu_line27, dependent.h_aoc_band]}

  - id: dependent.edu_line30
    printed: "30"
    label: "If line 28 is zero, enter the amount from line 27. Otherwise, add $2,000 to the amount on line 29 and enter the result"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [dependent.h_edu_first, dependent.edu_line29]}
    note: "Written as the smaller of line 27 or $2,000, plus line 29. When line 28 is zero, line 27 is $2,000 or less and line 29 is zero, so the result is line 27. Otherwise it is $2,000 plus line 29. This is exactly the printed rule."

  - id: dependent.edu_line31
    printed: "31"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (see instructions) (lifetime learning credit)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_llc_ok, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_edu_llc_adjusted}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_llc_ok, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}
    refuses: [dependent.h_edu_over]
```
```
